===== M0002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

/**
   * Creates a Scope given the parent Scope and the root node of the scope.
   * @param parent  The parent Scope. Cannot be null.
   * @param rootNode  Typically the FUNCTION node.
   */
Method code:
390 |   Scope(Scope parent, Node rootNode) {
391 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(parent);
392 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(rootNode != parent.rootNode);
393 | 
394 |     this.parent = parent;
395 |     this.rootNode = rootNode;
396 |     JSType nodeType = rootNode.getJSType();
397 |     if (nodeType != null && nodeType.isFunctionType()) {
398 |       thisType = nodeType.toMaybeFunctionType().getTypeOfThis();
399 |     } else {
400 |       thisType = parent.thisType;
401 |     }
402 |     this.isBottom = false;
403 |     this.depth = parent.depth + 1;
404 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock());
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

88 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.consume(List<PassFactory>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 113-134
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the passes generated by the given factories to the compile sequence.
   *
   * Automatically pulls multi-run passes into fixed point loops. If there
   * are 2 or more multi-run passes in a row, they will run together in
   * the same fixed point loop. If A and B are in the same fixed point loop,
   * the loop will continue to run both A and B until both are finished
   * making changes.
   *
   * Other than that, the PhaseOptimizer is free to tweak the order and
   * frequency of multi-run passes in a fixed-point loop.
   */
Method code:
113 |   void consume(List<PassFactory> factories) {
114 |     Loop currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
115 |     boolean isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
116 |     for (PassFactory factory : factories) {
117 |       if (factory.isOneTimePass()) {
118 |         if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
119 |           passes.add(currentLoop);
120 | 
121 |           currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
122 |           isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
123 |         }
124 |         addOneTimePass(factory);
125 |       } else {
126 |         currentLoop.addLoopedPass(factory);
127 |         isCurrentLoopPopulated = true;
128 |       }
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
132 |       passes.add(currentLoop);
133 |     }
134 |   }

337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }

347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 |

387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 227-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
227 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
228 |     this.options = options;
229 |     if (errorManager == null) {
230 |       if (outStream == null) {
231 |         setErrorManager(
232 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
233 |       } else {
234 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
235 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
236 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
237 |         setErrorManager(printer);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
242 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
243 |       options.checkTypes = true;
244 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
245 |       options.checkTypes = false;
246 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
247 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
248 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
249 |       // parser type warnings.
250 |       options.setWarningLevel(
251 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
252 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
253 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
257 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
258 |       options.setWarningLevel(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
260 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
261 |     }
262 | 
263 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
264 |       options.setWarningLevel(
265 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
266 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
270 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
271 |     guards.add(
272 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
273 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
274 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
275 | 
276 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
277 | 
278 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
279 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
280 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
281 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
282 |     // error off.
283 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
284 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
285 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
286 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
287 |     }
288 | 
289 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
290 |   }

166 |       case Token.POS:

170 |       case Token.BLOCK:

177 |                                           node.getClass() +

180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:

224 |                                           " type: " +

226 |         }

Method code:
302 |     public void collect(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
303 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
304 |         return;
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
308 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
309 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
310 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
311 |       }
312 | 
313 |       String name;
314 |       boolean isSet = false;
315 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
316 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
317 | 
318 |       switch (n.getType()) {
319 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
320 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
321 |         case Token.STRING:
322 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
323 |           name = null;
324 |           if (parent != null && parent.isObjectLit()) {
325 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
326 |           }
327 |           if (name == null) return;
328 |           isSet = true;
329 |           switch (n.getType()) {
330 |             case Token.STRING:
331 |               type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
332 |               break;
333 |             case Token.GETTER_DEF:
334 |               type = Name.Type.GET;
335 |               break;
336 |             case Token.SETTER_DEF:
337 |               type = Name.Type.SET;
338 |               break;
339 |             default:
340 |               throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected:" + n);
341 |           }
342 |           break;
343 |         case Token.NAME:
344 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
345 |           if (parent != null) {
346 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
347 |               case Token.VAR:
348 |                 isSet = true;
349 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
350 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
351 |                 break;
352 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
353 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
354 |                   isSet = true;
355 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
356 |                 }
357 |                 break;
358 |               case Token.GETPROP:
359 |                 return;
360 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
361 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
362 |                 if (gramps == null ||
363 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
364 |                 isSet = true;
365 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
366 |                 break;
367 |               case Token.INC:
368 |               case Token.DEC:
369 |                 isSet = true;
370 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
371 |                 break;
372 |               default:
373 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
374 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
375 |                   isSet = true;
376 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
377 |                 }
378 |             }
379 |           }
380 |           name = n.getString();
381 |           break;
382 |         case Token.GETPROP:
383 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
384 |           if (parent != null) {
385 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
386 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
387 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
388 |                   isSet = true;
389 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
390 |                   isPropAssign = true;
391 |                 }
392 |                 break;
393 |               case Token.INC:
394 |               case Token.DEC:
395 |                 isSet = true;
396 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
397 |                 break;
398 |               case Token.GETPROP:
399 |                 return;
400 |               default:
401 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
402 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
403 |                   isSet = true;
404 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
405 |                 }
406 |             }
407 |           }
408 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
409 |           if (name == null) return;
410 |           break;
411 |         default:
412 |           return;
413 |       }
414 | 
415 |       // We are only interested in global names.
416 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
417 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
418 |         return;
419 |       }
420 | 
421 |       if (isSet) {
422 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
423 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
424 |         } else {
425 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
426 |         }
427 |       } else {
428 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
429 |       }
430 |     }

/**
     * Determines whether assignment to a define should be allowed
     * in the subtree of the given node, and if not, records that fact.
     *
     * @param n The node whose subtree we're about to enter or exit.
     * @param entering True if we're entering the subtree, false otherwise.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private void updateAssignAllowedStack(Node n, boolean entering) {
381 |       switch (n.getType()) {
382 |         case Token.CASE:
383 |         case Token.FOR:
384 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
385 |         case Token.HOOK:
386 |         case Token.IF:
387 |         case Token.SWITCH:
388 |         case Token.WHILE:
389 |           if (entering) {
390 |             assignAllowed.push(0);
391 |           } else {
392 |             assignAllowed.remove();
393 |           }
394 |           break;
395 |       }
396 |     }

/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line and all subsequent
   * until either an annotation, end of comment or end of file is reached.
   * Note that if this method detects an end of line as the first token, it
   * will quit immediately (indicating that there is no text where it was
   * expected).  Note that token = info.token; should be called after this
   * method is used to update the token properly in the parser.
   *
   * @param token The start token.
   * @param option How to handle whitespace.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1319 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
1320 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1321 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1322 | 
1323 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1324 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1325 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1326 |     }
1327 | 
1328 |     stream.update();
1329 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1330 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1331 | 
1332 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1333 |     String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1334 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1335 |       line = line.trim();
1336 |     }
1337 | 
1338 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1339 |     builder.append(line);
1340 | 
1341 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1342 |     token = next();
1343 | 
1344 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1345 | 
1346 |     do {
1347 |       switch (token) {
1348 |         case STAR:
1349 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1350 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1351 |               builder.append(' ');
1352 |             }
1353 | 
1354 |             builder.append('*');
1355 |           }
1356 | 
1357 |           token = next();
1358 |           continue;
1359 | 
1360 |         case EOL:
1361 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1362 |             builder.append("\n");
1363 |           }
1364 | 
1365 |           ignoreStar = true;
1366 |           token = next();
1367 |           continue;
1368 | 
1369 |         case ANNOTATION:
1370 |         case EOC:
1371 |         case EOF:
1372 |           // When we're capturing a license block, annotations
1373 |           // in the block are ok.
1374 |           if (!(option == WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE &&
1375 |                 token == JsDocToken.ANNOTATION)) {
1376 |             String multilineText = builder.toString();
1377 | 
1378 |             if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1379 |               multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1380 |             }
1381 | 
1382 |             int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1383 |             int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1384 | 
1385 |             if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1386 |               jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1387 |                   endLineno, endCharno);
1388 |             }
1389 | 
1390 |             return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1391 |           }
1392 | 
1393 |           // FALL THROUGH
1394 | 
1395 |         default:
1396 |           ignoreStar = false;
1397 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1398 | 
1399 |           if (builder.length() > 0) {
1400 |             builder.append(' ');
1401 |           }
1402 | 
1403 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1404 | 
1405 |           line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1406 | 
1407 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1408 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1409 |           }
1410 | 
1411 |           builder.append(line);
1412 |           token = next();
1413 |       }
1414 |     } while (true);
1415 |   }

1709 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1710 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1711 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.

1714 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1715 |       case Token.BLOCK:

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
180 |   boolean parse() {
181 |     int lineno;
182 |     int charno;
183 | 
184 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
185 |     JSTypeExpression type;
186 | 
187 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
188 |     skipEOLs();
189 | 
190 |     JsDocToken token = next();
191 | 
192 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
193 | 
194 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
195 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
196 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
197 |       token = blockInfo.token;
198 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
199 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
200 |       }
201 |     } else {
202 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
203 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
204 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
205 |         // what it was.
206 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
207 |       }
208 |     }
209 | 
210 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
211 |     retry: for (;;) {
212 |       switch (token) {
213 |         case ANNOTATION:
214 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
215 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
216 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
217 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
218 | 
219 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
220 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
221 |             if (annotation == null) {
222 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
223 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
224 |             } else {
225 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
226 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
227 | 
228 |               switch (annotation) {
229 |                 case AUTHOR:
230 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
231 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
232 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;

234 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
235 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
236 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
237 |                     } else {

239 |                     }
240 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
241 |                   } else {
242 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
243 |                   }
244 |                   continue retry;
245 | 
246 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
247 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
248 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
249 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
250 |                   }
251 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
252 |                   continue retry;
253 | 
254 |                 case CONSTANT:
255 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
256 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
257 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
258 |                   }
259 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
260 |                   continue retry;
261 | 
262 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
263 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
264 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
265 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
266 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

269 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
270 |                     }

273 |                   continue retry;
274 | 
275 |                 case DEPRECATED:
276 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
277 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
278 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
279 |                   }
280 | 
281 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
282 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
283 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
284 | 
285 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
286 | 
287 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
288 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
289 |                   }
290 | 
291 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
292 |                   continue retry;
293 | 
294 |                 case INTERFACE:
295 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
296 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
297 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
298 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
299 |                     } else {
300 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",

302 |                     }

305 |                   continue retry;

490 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
491 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
492 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                   }
494 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
495 |                   continue retry;
496 | 
497 |                 case MEANING:
498 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
499 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
500 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
501 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
502 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
503 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
504 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
505 |                   }
506 |                   continue retry;
507 | 
508 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
509 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
510 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
511 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
512 |                   }
513 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
514 |                   continue retry;
515 | 
516 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
517 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
518 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
519 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
520 |                   }
521 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
522 |                   continue retry;
523 | 
524 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
525 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
526 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
527 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
528 |                   }
529 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
530 |                   continue retry;
531 | 
532 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
533 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
534 |                   continue retry;
535 | 
536 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
537 |                 case OVERRIDE:
538 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
539 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
540 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
541 |                   }
542 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
543 |                   continue retry;
544 | 
545 |                 case THROWS:
546 |                   skipEOLs();
547 |                   token = next();
548 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
549 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
550 |                   type = null;
551 | 
552 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
553 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
554 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
555 | 
556 |                     if (type == null) {
557 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
558 |                       // recovering parsing
559 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
560 |                       continue retry;
561 |                     }
562 |                   }
563 | 
564 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
565 |                   token = current();
566 | 
567 |                   // Save the throw type.
568 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
569 | 
570 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
571 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
572 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
573 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
574 | 
575 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
576 | 
577 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
578 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
579 |                     }
580 | 
581 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
582 |                   } else {
583 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
584 |                   }
585 |                   continue retry;
586 | 
587 |                 case PARAM:
588 |                   skipEOLs();
589 |                   token = next();
590 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
591 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
592 |                   type = null;
593 | 
594 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
595 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
596 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
597 | 
598 |                     if (type == null) {
599 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
600 |                       // recovering parsing
601 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
602 |                       continue retry;
603 |                     }
604 |                     skipEOLs();
605 |                     token = next();
606 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
607 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
608 |                   }
609 | 
610 |                   String name = null;
611 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
612 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
613 |                     token = next();
614 |                   }
615 | 
616 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
617 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
618 |                         lineno, charno);
619 |                   } else {
620 |                     name = stream.getString();
621 | 
622 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
623 |                       token = next();
624 | 
625 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
626 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
627 |                       // system.
628 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
629 |                         token = next();
630 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
631 |                           token = next();
632 |                         }
633 |                       }
634 | 
635 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
636 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
637 |                       } else if (type != null) {
638 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
639 |                         // already.
640 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
641 |                       }
642 |                     }
643 | 
644 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
645 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
646 |                     // for handling properties of params.
647 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
648 |                       name = null;
649 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
650 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
651 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
652 |                             lineno, charno);
653 |                       } else {
654 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
655 |                             lineno, charno);
656 |                       }
657 |                     }
658 |                   }
659 | 
660 |                   if (name == null) {
661 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
662 |                     continue retry;
663 |                   }
664 | 
665 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, sourceFile, lineno, charno);
666 | 
667 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
668 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
669 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
670 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
671 | 
672 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
673 | 
674 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
675 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
676 |                           paramDescription);
677 |                     }
678 | 
679 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
680 |                   } else {
681 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
682 |                   }
683 |                   continue retry;
684 | 
685 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
686 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
687 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
688 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
689 |                   }
690 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
691 |                   continue retry;
692 | 
693 |                 case PRIVATE:
694 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
695 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
696 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
697 |                   }
698 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
699 |                   continue retry;
700 | 
701 |                 case PROTECTED:
702 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
703 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
704 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
705 |                   }
706 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
707 |                   continue retry;
708 | 
709 |                 case PUBLIC:
710 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
711 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
712 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
713 |                   }
714 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
715 |                   continue retry;
716 | 
717 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
718 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
719 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
720 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
721 |                   }
722 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
723 |                   continue retry;
724 | 
725 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
726 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
727 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
728 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
729 |                   }
730 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
731 |                   continue retry;
732 | 
733 |                 case MODIFIES:
734 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
735 |                   continue retry;
736 | 
737 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
738 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
739 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
740 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
741 |                   }
742 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
743 |                   continue retry;
744 | 
745 |                 case SEE:
746 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
747 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
748 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
749 | 
750 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
751 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
752 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
753 |                     } else {
754 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
755 |                     }
756 | 
757 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
758 |                   } else {
759 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
760 |                   }
761 |                   continue retry;
762 | 
763 |                 case SUPPRESS:
764 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
765 |                   continue retry;
766 | 
767 |                 case TEMPLATE:
768 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
769 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
770 | 
771 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
772 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
773 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
774 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
775 |                       templateTypeName)) {
776 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
777 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
778 |                   }
779 | 
780 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
781 |                   continue retry;
782 | 
783 |                 case IDGENERATOR:
784 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordIdGenerator()) {
785 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.idgen",
786 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
787 |                   }
788 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
789 |                   continue retry;
790 | 
791 |                 case VERSION:
792 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
793 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
794 | 
795 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
796 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
797 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
798 |                   } else {
799 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
800 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
801 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
802 |                     }
803 |                   }
804 | 
805 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
806 |                   continue retry;
807 | 
808 |                 case DEFINE:
809 |                 case RETURN:
810 |                 case THIS:
811 |                 case TYPE:
812 |                 case TYPEDEF:
813 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
814 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
815 | 
816 |                   Node typeNode = null;
817 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
818 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
819 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
820 |                     // it as the unknown type.
821 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
822 |                   } else {
823 |                     skipEOLs();
824 |                     token = next();
825 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
826 |                   }
827 | 
828 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
829 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
830 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
831 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
832 |                     }
833 |                   }
834 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
835 | 
836 |                   if (type == null) {
837 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
838 |                     // recovering parsing
839 |                   } else {
840 |                     switch (annotation) {
841 |                       case DEFINE:
842 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
843 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
844 |                               lineno, charno);
845 |                         }
846 |                         break;
847 | 
848 |                       case RETURN:
849 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
850 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
851 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
852 |                           break;
853 |                         }
854 | 
855 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
856 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
857 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
858 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
859 | 
860 |                           String returnDescription =
861 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
862 | 
863 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
864 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
865 |                                 returnDescription);
866 |                           }
867 | 
868 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
869 |                         } else {
870 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
871 |                         }
872 |                         continue retry;
873 | 
874 |                       case THIS:
875 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
876 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
877 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
878 |                         }
879 |                         break;
880 | 
881 |                       case TYPE:
882 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
883 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
884 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
885 |                         }
886 |                         break;
887 | 
888 |                       case TYPEDEF:
889 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
890 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
891 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
892 |                         }
893 |                         break;
894 |                     }
895 |                   }
896 | 
897 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
898 |                   continue retry;
899 |               }
900 |             }
901 |           }
902 |           break;
903 | 
904 |         case EOC:
905 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
906 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
907 |           }
908 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
909 |           return true;
910 | 
911 |         case EOF:
912 |           // discard any accumulated information
913 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
914 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
915 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
916 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
917 |           return false;
918 | 
919 |         case EOL:
920 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
921 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
922 |           }
923 |           token = next();
924 |           continue retry;
925 | 
926 |         default:
927 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
928 |             token = next();
929 |             continue retry;
930 |           } else {
931 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
932 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
933 |             continue retry;
934 |           }
935 |       }
936 | 
937 |       // next token
938 |       token = next();
939 |     }
940 |   }

Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapConsumerV3.visitMappings(EntryVisitor)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapConsumerV3.java
Original range: 684-723
Documentation:

Method code:
684 |   public void visitMappings(EntryVisitor visitor) {
685 |     boolean pending = false;
686 |     String sourceName = null;
687 |     String symbolName = null;
688 |     FilePosition sourceStartPosition = null;
689 |     FilePosition startPosition = null;
690 | 
691 |     final int lineCount = lines.size();
692 |     for (int i = 0; i < lineCount; i++) {
693 |       ArrayList<Entry> line = lines.get(i);
694 |       if (line != null) {
695 |         final int entryCount = line.size();
696 |         for (int j = 0; j < entryCount; j++) {
697 |           Entry entry = line.get(j);
698 |           if (pending) {
699 |             FilePosition endPosition = new FilePosition(
700 |                 i, entry.getGeneratedColumn());
701 |             visitor.visit(
702 |                 sourceName,
703 |                 symbolName,
704 |                 sourceStartPosition,
705 |                 startPosition,
706 |                 endPosition);
707 |             pending = false;
708 |           }
709 | 
710 |           if (entry.getSourceFileId() != UNMAPPED) {
711 |             pending = true;
712 |             sourceName = sources[entry.getSourceFileId()];
713 |             symbolName = (entry.getNameId() != UNMAPPED)
714 |                 ? names[entry.getNameId()] : null;
715 |             sourceStartPosition = new FilePosition(
716 |                 entry.getSourceLine(), entry.getSourceColumn());
717 |             startPosition = new FilePosition(
718 |                 i, entry.getGeneratedColumn());
719 |           }
720 |         }
721 |       }
722 |     }
723 |   }

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runCallable(Callable<T>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 580-632
Documentation:

Method code:
580 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
581 |   static <T> T runCallable(
582 |       final Callable<T> callable, boolean useLargeStackThread, boolean trace) {
583 | 
584 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the jscompiler overflows the stack when running on some
585 |     // large or complex js code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
586 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
587 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
588 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
589 | 
590 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = trace;
591 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
592 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
593 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
594 |       @Override
595 |       public void run() {
596 |         try {
597 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
598 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
599 |           }
600 |           result[0] = callable.call();
601 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
602 |           exception[0] = e;
603 |         } finally {
604 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
605 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
606 |           }
607 |         }
608 |       }
609 |     };
610 | 
611 |     if (useLargeStackThread) {
612 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
613 |       th.start();
614 |       while (true) {
615 |         try {
616 |           th.join();
617 |           break;
618 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
619 |           // ignore
620 |         }
621 |       }
622 |     } else {
623 |       runnable.run();
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
627 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
628 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     return (T) result[0];
632 |   }

1466 |       public Void call() throws Exception {

1469 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line

1491 |             .append("*/\n");

1506 |

1508 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1509 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1510 |           int length = code.length();
1511 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1512 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1513 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1514 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1515 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1516 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1517 |             cb.append(";");
1518 |           }
1519 |         }
1520 |         return null;
1521 |       }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

===== M0095 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1905-1907
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1905 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1906 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1907 |   }
