===== M0110 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-716
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
104 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
105 | 
106 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
107 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
108 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
109 | 
110 |       // Handle associativity.
111 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
112 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
113 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
114 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
119 |       } else {
120 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
121 |       }
122 |       return;
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
126 | 
127 |     switch (type) {
128 |       case Token.TRY: {
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
130 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
132 | 
133 |         add("try");
134 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
135 | 
136 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
137 |         // isn't a catch block
138 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
139 |         if (catchblock != null) {
140 |           add(catchblock);
141 |         }
142 | 
143 |         if (childCount == 3) {
144 |           add("finally");
145 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         }
147 |         break;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       case Token.CATCH:
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
152 |         add("catch(");
153 |         add(first);
154 |         add(")");
155 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.THROW:
159 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
160 |         add("throw");
161 |         add(first);
162 | 
163 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
164 |         // parse this.
165 |         cc.endStatement(true);
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.RETURN:
169 |         add("return");
170 |         if (childCount == 1) {
171 |           add(first);
172 |         } else {
173 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
174 |         }
175 |         cc.endStatement();
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.VAR:
179 |         if (first != null) {
180 |           add("var ");
181 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
182 |         }
183 |         break;
184 | 
185 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
186 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
187 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
188 |         break;
189 | 
190 |       case Token.NAME:
191 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |         } else {
194 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
195 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
196 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
197 |           if (first.isComma()) {
198 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
199 |           } else {
200 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
201 |             // precedence.
202 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
203 |           }
204 |         }
205 |         break;
206 | 
207 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
208 |         add("[");
209 |         addArrayList(first);
210 |         add("]");
211 |         break;
212 | 
213 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
214 |         add("(");
215 |         addList(first);
216 |         add(")");
217 |         break;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.COMMA:
220 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
221 |         addList(first, false, context);
222 |         break;
223 | 
224 |       case Token.NUMBER:
225 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
270 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
286 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
287 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
288 |         }
289 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
290 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
291 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
292 |           add("(");
293 |         }
294 | 
295 |         add("function");
296 |         add(first);
297 | 
298 |         add(first.getNext());
299 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
300 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
301 | 
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add(")");
304 |         }
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
308 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
309 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
310 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
312 | 
313 |         // Get methods are unnamed
314 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
315 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
316 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
317 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
318 |           add("get ");
319 |         } else {
320 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
321 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
322 |           add("set ");
323 |         }
324 | 
325 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
326 |         String name = n.getString();
327 |         Node fn = first;
328 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
329 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
330 | 
331 |         // Add the property name.
332 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
333 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
334 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
335 |             // unicode escaped.
336 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
337 |           add(name);
338 |         } else {
339 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
340 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
341 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
342 |             cc.addNumber(d);
343 |           } else {
344 |             addJsString(n.getString());
345 |           }
346 |         }
347 | 
348 |         add(parameters);
349 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
350 |         break;
351 | 
352 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
353 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
354 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
355 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
356 |         }
357 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
358 |         if (preserveBlock) {
359 |           cc.beginBlock();
360 |         }
361 | 
362 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
363 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
364 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
365 |                 !preserveBlock &&
366 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
367 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
368 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
369 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
370 | 
371 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
372 |           if (c.isVar()) {
373 |             cc.endStatement();
374 |           }
375 | 
376 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
377 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
378 |           }
379 | 
380 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
381 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
382 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
383 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
384 |           }
385 |         }
386 |         if (preserveBlock) {
387 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
388 |         }
389 |         break;
390 |       }
391 | 
392 |       case Token.FOR:
393 |         if (childCount == 4) {
394 |           add("for(");
395 |           if (first.isVar()) {
396 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
397 |           } else {
398 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           }
400 |           add(";");
401 |           add(first.getNext());
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
404 |           add(")");
405 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
406 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
407 |         } else {
408 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
409 |           add("for(");
410 |           add(first);
411 |           add("in");
412 |           add(first.getNext());
413 |           add(")");
414 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
415 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
416 |         }
417 |         break;
418 | 
419 |       case Token.DO:
420 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
421 |         add("do");
422 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
423 |         add("while(");
424 |         add(last);
425 |         add(")");
426 |         cc.endStatement();
427 |         break;
428 | 
429 |       case Token.WHILE:
430 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
431 |         add("while(");
432 |         add(first);
433 |         add(")");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
435 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
436 |         break;
437 | 
438 |       case Token.EMPTY:
439 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
440 |         break;
441 | 
442 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(
444 |             childCount == 2,
445 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
446 |         Preconditions.checkState(
447 |             last.isString(),
448 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
449 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
450 |         if (needsParens) {
451 |           add("(");
452 |         }
453 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
454 |         if (needsParens) {
455 |           add(")");
456 |         }
457 |         add(".");
458 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
459 |         break;
460 |       }
461 | 
462 |       case Token.GETELEM:
463 |         Preconditions.checkState(
464 |             childCount == 2,
465 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
466 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
467 |         add("[");
468 |         add(first.getNext());
469 |         add("]");
470 |         break;
471 | 
472 |       case Token.WITH:
473 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
474 |         add("with(");
475 |         add(first);
476 |         add(")");
477 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
478 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
479 |         break;
480 | 
481 |       case Token.INC:
482 |       case Token.DEC: {
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
484 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
485 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
486 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
487 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
488 |         if (postProp != 0) {
489 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
490 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
491 |         } else {
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |           add(first);
494 |         }
495 |         break;
496 |       }
497 | 
498 |       case Token.CALL:
499 |         // We have two special cases here:
500 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
501 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
502 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
503 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
504 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
505 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
506 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
507 |         // that must be preserved.
508 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
509 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
510 |           add("(0,");
511 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
512 |           add(")");
513 |         } else {
514 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
515 |         }
516 |         add("(");
517 |         addList(first.getNext());
518 |         add(")");
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.IF:
522 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
523 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
524 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
525 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
526 |           cc.beginBlock();
527 |         }
528 | 
529 |         add("if(");
530 |         add(first);
531 |         add(")");
532 | 
533 |         if (hasElse) {
534 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
535 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
536 |           add("else");
537 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
538 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
539 |         } else {
540 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
541 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
542 |         }
543 | 
544 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
545 |           cc.endBlock();
546 |         }
547 |         break;
548 | 
549 |       case Token.NULL:
550 |       case Token.THIS:
551 |       case Token.FALSE:
552 |       case Token.TRUE:
553 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
554 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
555 |         break;
556 | 
557 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
558 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
559 |         add("continue");
560 |         if (childCount == 1) {
561 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
562 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
563 |           }
564 |           add(" ");
565 |           add(first);
566 |         }
567 |         cc.endStatement();
568 |         break;
569 | 
570 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
571 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
572 |         add("debugger");
573 |         cc.endStatement();
574 |         break;
575 | 
576 |       case Token.BREAK:
577 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
578 |         add("break");
579 |         if (childCount == 1) {
580 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
581 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
582 |           }
583 |           add(" ");
584 |           add(first);
585 |         }
586 |         cc.endStatement();
587 |         break;
588 | 
589 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
590 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
591 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
592 |         cc.endStatement();
593 |         break;
594 | 
595 |       case Token.NEW:
596 |         add("new ");
597 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
598 | 
599 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
600 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
601 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
602 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
603 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
604 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
605 |         }
606 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
607 | 
608 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
609 |         Node next = first.getNext();
610 |         if (next != null) {
611 |           add("(");
612 |           addList(next);
613 |           add(")");
614 |         }
615 |         break;
616 | 
617 |       case Token.STRING:
618 |         if (childCount !=
619 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
620 |               n.getParent().isObjectLit()) ? 1 : 0)) {
621 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
622 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
623 |         }
624 |         addJsString(n.getString());
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.DELPROP:
628 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
629 |         add("delete ");
630 |         add(first);
631 |         break;
632 | 
633 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
634 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
635 |         if (needsParens) {
636 |           add("(");
637 |         }
638 |         add("{");
639 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
640 |           if (c != first) {
641 |             cc.listSeparator();
642 |           }
643 | 
644 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
645 |             add(c);
646 |           } else {
647 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isString());
648 |             String key = c.getString();
649 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
650 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
651 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
652 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
653 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
654 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
655 |                 // were unicode escaped.
656 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
657 |               add(key);
658 |             } else {
659 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
660 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
661 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
662 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
663 |               } else {
664 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
665 |               }
666 |             }
667 |             add(":");
668 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
669 |           }
670 |         }
671 |         add("}");
672 |         if (needsParens) {
673 |           add(")");
674 |         }
675 |         break;
676 |       }
677 | 
678 |       case Token.SWITCH:
679 |         add("switch(");
680 |         add(first);
681 |         add(")");
682 |         cc.beginBlock();
683 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
684 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
685 |         break;
686 | 
687 |       case Token.CASE:
688 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
689 |         add("case ");
690 |         add(first);
691 |         addCaseBody(last);
692 |         break;
693 | 
694 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
695 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
696 |         add("default");
697 |         addCaseBody(first);
698 |         break;
699 | 
700 |       case Token.LABEL:
701 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
702 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
703 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
704 |         }
705 |         add(first);
706 |         add(":");
707 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
708 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
709 |         break;
710 | 
711 |       default:
712 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
713 |     }
714 | 
715 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
716 |   }

Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapGeneratorV3.prepMappings()
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapGeneratorV3.java
Original range: 378-397
Documentation:
/**
   * Assigns sequential ids to used mappings, and returns the last line mapped.
   */
Method code:
378 |   private int prepMappings() throws IOException {
379 |     // Mark any unused mappings.
380 |     (new MappingTraversal()).traverse(new UsedMappingCheck());
381 | 
382 |     // Renumber used mappings and keep track of the last line.
383 |     int id = 0;
384 |     int maxLine = 0;
385 |     int sourceId = 0;
386 |     int nameId = 0;
387 |     for (Mapping m : mappings) {
388 |       if (m.used) {
389 |         m.id = id++;
390 |         int endPositionLine = m.endPosition.getLine();
391 |         maxLine = Math.max(maxLine, endPositionLine);
392 |       }
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     // Adjust for the prefix.
396 |     return maxLine + prefixPosition.getLine();
397 |   }

===== M0123 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(Comment,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 334-354
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.
   * @param irNode
   * @return A JSDocInfoParser. Will contain either fileoverview jsdoc, or
   *     normal jsdoc, or no jsdoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).
   */
Method code:
334 |   private JsDocInfoParser createJsDocInfoParser(Comment node, Node irNode) {
335 |     String comment = node.getValue();
336 |     int lineno = node.getLineno();
337 |     int position = node.getAbsolutePosition();
338 | 
339 |     // The JsDocInfoParser expects the comment without the initial '/**'.
340 |     int numOpeningChars = 3;
341 |     JsDocInfoParser jsdocParser =
342 |       new JsDocInfoParser(
343 |           new JsDocTokenStream(comment.substring(numOpeningChars),
344 |                                lineno,
345 |                                position2charno(position) + numOpeningChars),
346 |           node,
347 |           irNode,
348 |           config,
349 |           errorReporter);
350 |     jsdocParser.setFileLevelJsDocBuilder(fileLevelJsDocBuilder);
351 |     jsdocParser.setFileOverviewJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
352 |     jsdocParser.parse();
353 |     return jsdocParser;
354 |   }

===== M0125 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.isGlobalNameReference(String,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 229-232
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a name reference in a particular scope is a global name
   * reference.
   *

===== M0129 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

/**

     */
Method code:
582 |     private void visit(MappingVisitor v, Mapping m,
583 |         int nextLine, int nextCol)
584 |         throws IOException {
585 |       Preconditions.checkState(line <= nextLine);

237 |     CollectDefines(AbstractCompiler compiler, List<Name> listOfDefines) {
238 |       this.compiler = compiler;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)

Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

595 |   public Scope getScope() {

Method code:
123 |   private void overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo> allDefines) {
124 |     boolean changed = false;
125 |     for (Map.Entry<String, DefineInfo> def : allDefines.entrySet()) {
126 |       String defineName = def.getKey();
127 |       DefineInfo info = def.getValue();
128 |       Node inputValue = dominantReplacements.get(defineName);
129 |       Node finalValue = inputValue != null ?
130 |           inputValue : info.getLastValue();
131 |       if (finalValue != info.initialValue) {
132 |         info.initialValueParent.replaceChild(
133 |             info.initialValue, finalValue.cloneTree());
134 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("Overriding @define variable " + defineName);
135 |         changed = changed ||
136 |             finalValue.getType() != info.initialValue.getType() ||
137 |             !finalValue.isEquivalentTo(info.initialValue);
138 |       }
139 |     }
140 | 
141 |     if (changed) {
142 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     Set<String> unusedReplacements = dominantReplacements.keySet();
146 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(allDefines.keySet());
147 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(KNOWN_DEFINES);
148 |     for (String unknownDefine : unusedReplacements) {
149 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(UNKNOWN_DEFINE_WARNING, unknownDefine));
150 |     }
151 |   }

   * Marks the end of a pass.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void endPass(Node externs, Node root) {
188 |     Preconditions.checkState(currentTracer != null && currentPassName != null);
189 | 
190 |     String passToCheck = currentPassName;
191 |     try {
192 |       stopTracer(currentTracer, currentPassName);
193 |       currentPassName = null;
194 |       currentTracer = null;
195 | 
196 |       maybeSanityCheck(externs, root);
197 |     } catch (Exception e) {
198 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Remove this once the normalization checks report
199 |       // errors instead of exceptions.
200 |       throw new RuntimeException("Sanity check failed for " + passToCheck, e);
201 |     }
202 |   }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 782-820
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
782 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
783 |         String name) {
784 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
785 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
786 |       // assignments.
787 | 
788 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
789 |       String prefix;
790 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
791 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
792 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
793 |       } else {
794 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
795 |         if (i == -1) {
796 |           return false;
797 |         }
798 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
799 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
800 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
801 |         while (i >= 0) {
802 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
803 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
804 |         }
805 |       }
806 | 
807 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
808 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
809 |         // key, so we're done.
810 |         return true;
811 |       }
812 | 
813 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
814 |         parent = n;
815 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
816 |       }
817 | 
818 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
819 |       return true;
820 |     }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1058-1078
Documentation:

Method code:
1058 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1059 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1060 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1061 |       return s;
1062 |     }
1063 | 
1064 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1065 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1066 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1067 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1068 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1069 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1070 |       // crazy.
1071 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1072 |         sb.append(c);
1073 |       } else {
1074 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1075 |       }
1076 |     }
1077 |     return sb.toString();
1078 |   }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.Base64VLQ.decode(CharIterator)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/Base64VLQ.java
Original range: 112-126
Documentation:
/**
   * Decodes the next VLQValue from the provided CharIterator.
   */
Method code:
112 |   public static int decode(CharIterator in) {
113 |     int result = 0;
114 |     boolean continuation;
115 |     int shift = 0;
116 |     do {
117 |       char c = in.next();
118 |       int digit = Base64.fromBase64(c);
119 |       continuation = (digit & VLQ_CONTINUATION_BIT) != 0;
120 |       digit &= VLQ_BASE_MASK;
121 |       result = result + (digit << shift);
122 |       shift = shift + VLQ_BASE_SHIFT;
123 |     } while (continuation);
124 | 
125 |     return fromVLQSigned(result);
126 |   }

===== M0249 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SourceInformationAnnotator.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SourceInformationAnnotator.java
Original range: 53-89
Documentation:

Method code:
53 |   @Override
54 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
55 |     // Verify the source file is annotated.
56 |     if (doSanityChecks && sourceFile != null) {
57 |       Preconditions.checkState(sourceFile.equals(
58 |           n.getSourceFileName()));
59 |     }
60 | 
61 |     // Annotate the original name.
62 |     switch (n.getType()) {
63 |       case Token.GETPROP:
64 |         Node propNode = n.getLastChild();
65 |         setOriginalName(n, propNode.getString());
66 |         break;
67 | 
68 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
69 |         String functionName = NodeUtil.getNearestFunctionName(n);
70 |         if (functionName != null) {
71 |           setOriginalName(n, functionName);
72 |         }
73 |         break;
74 | 
75 |       case Token.NAME:
76 |         setOriginalName(n, n.getString());
77 |         break;
78 | 
79 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
80 |         for (Node key = n.getFirstChild(); key != null;
81 |              key = key.getNext()) {
82 |            // We only want keys were unquoted.
83 |            if (!key.isQuotedString()) {
84 |              setOriginalName(key, key.getString());
85 |            }
86 |          }
87 |         break;
88 |     }
89 |   }

/**
     * Starts the source mapping for the given
     * node at the current position.
     */
Method code:
89 |     @Override
90 |     void startSourceMapping(Node node) {
91 |       Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
92 |       Preconditions.checkState(node != null);
93 |       if (createSrcMap
94 |           && node.getSourceFileName() != null
95 |           && node.getLineno() > 0
96 |           && sourceMapDetailLevel.apply(node)) {
97 |         int line = getCurrentLineIndex();
98 |         int index = getCurrentCharIndex();
99 |         Preconditions.checkState(line >= 0);
100 |         Mapping mapping = new Mapping();
101 |         mapping.node = node;
102 |         mapping.start = new FilePosition(line, index);
103 |         mappings.push(mapping);
104 |         allMappings.add(mapping);
105 |       }
106 |     }

===== M0252 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.createSynthesizedExternVar(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 219-236
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new variable in a synthetic script. This will prevent
   * subsequent compiler passes from crashing.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private void createSynthesizedExternVar(String varName) {
220 |     Node nameNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, varName);
221 | 
222 |     // Mark the variable as constant if it matches the coding convention
223 |     // for constant vars.
224 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): honestly, i'm not sure how much this matters.
225 |     // AFAIK, all people who use the CONST coding convention also
226 |     // compile with undeclaredVars as errors. We have some test
227 |     // cases for this configuration though, and it makes them happier.
228 |     if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isConstant(varName)) {
229 |       nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
230 |     }
231 | 
232 |     getSynthesizedExternsRoot().addChildToBack(
233 |         new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode));
234 |     varsToDeclareInExterns.remove(varName);
235 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
236 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.Util.escapeString(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/Util.java
Original range: 38-107
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
38 |   static String escapeString(String s, char quote,
39 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
40 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
41 |                           String backslashEscape,
42 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
43 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
44 |     sb.append(quote);
45 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
46 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
47 |       switch (c) {
48 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
49 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
50 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
51 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
52 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
53 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
54 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
55 |           if (i >= 2 &&
56 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
57 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
58 |             sb.append("\\>");
59 |           } else {
60 |             sb.append(c);
61 |           }
62 |           break;
63 |         case '<':
64 |           // Break </script into <\/script
65 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
66 | 
67 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
68 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
69 | 
70 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
71 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
72 |             sb.append("<\\");
73 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
74 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
75 |             sb.append("<\\");
76 |           } else {
77 |             sb.append(c);
78 |           }
79 |           break;
80 |         default:
81 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
82 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
83 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
84 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
85 |               sb.append(c);
86 |             } else {
87 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
88 |               appendCharAsHex(sb, c);
89 |             }
90 |           } else {
91 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
92 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
93 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
94 |             if (c > 0x1f && c <= 0x7f) {
95 |               sb.append(c);
96 |             } else {
97 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
98 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
99 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
100 |               appendCharAsHex(sb, c);
101 |             }
102 |           }
103 |       }
104 |     }
105 |     sb.append(quote);
106 |     return sb.toString();
107 |   }

===== M0263 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 331-358
Documentation:

Method code:
331 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
332 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
333 |     // two cases:
334 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
335 |     // and
336 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
337 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
338 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
339 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
340 |     //
341 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
342 |     //
343 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
344 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
345 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
346 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
347 |     //
348 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
349 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
350 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
351 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
352 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
353 | 
354 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
355 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
356 |     }
357 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
358 |   }

===== M0270 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 599-657
Documentation:

Method code:
599 |     @Override
600 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
601 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
602 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
603 |       if (name == null) {
604 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
605 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
606 |           errorReporter.error(
607 |             "unnamed function statement",
608 |             sourceName,
609 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
610 |         }
611 |         name = new Name();
612 |         name.setIdentifier("");
613 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
614 |       }
615 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
616 |       Node newName = transform(name);
617 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
618 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
619 |         // declaration.
620 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
621 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
622 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
623 |         // same line as the function keyword.
624 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
625 |             functionNode.getLp();
626 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
627 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
628 |       }
629 | 
630 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
631 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
632 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
633 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
634 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
635 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
636 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
637 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
638 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
639 |       if (fnName != null) {
640 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
641 |       } else {
642 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
643 |       }
644 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
645 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
646 | 
647 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
648 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
649 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
650 |       }
651 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
652 | 
653 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
654 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
655 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
656 |      return node;
657 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 634-674
Documentation:

Method code:
634 |   private void compileInternal() {
635 |     parse();
636 |     if (hasErrors()) {
637 |       return;
638 |     }
639 | 
640 |     if (!precheck()) {
641 |       return;
642 |     }
643 | 
644 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
645 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
646 |       check();
647 |       return;
648 |     }
649 | 
650 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
651 |       check();
652 |       if (hasErrors()) {
653 |         return;
654 |       }
655 | 
656 |       if (options.isExternExportsEnabled()
657 |           || options.externExportsPath != null) {
658 |         externExports();
659 |       }
660 | 
661 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
662 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
663 |         optimize();
664 |       }
665 |     }
666 | 
667 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
668 |       recordFunctionInformation();
669 |     }
670 | 
671 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
672 |       runSanityCheck();
673 |     }
674 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getRemainingJSDocLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 201-225
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the remaining JSDoc line without the {@link JsDocToken#EOL},
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOF} or {@link JsDocToken#EOC}.
   */
Method code:
201 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
202 |   String getRemainingJSDocLine() {
203 |     int c;
204 |     for (;;) {
205 |       c = getChar();
206 |       switch (c) {
207 |         case '*':
208 |           if (peekChar() != '/') {
209 |             addToString(c);
210 |             break;
211 |           }
212 |           // fall through
213 |         case EOF_CHAR:
214 |         case '\n':
215 |           ungetChar(c);
216 |           this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
217 |           stringBufferTop = 0;
218 |           return this.string;
219 | 
220 |         default:
221 |           addToString(c);
222 |           break;
223 |       }
224 |     }
225 |   }

===== M0292 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 457-472
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
457 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
458 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
459 |       Set<String> directives = null;
460 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
461 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
462 |         if (directives == null) {
463 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
464 |         } else {
465 |           directives.add(directive);
466 |         }
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (directives != null) {
470 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
471 |       }
472 |     }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.

103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0312 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapGeneratorV3.appendTo(Appendable,String)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapGeneratorV3.java
Original range: 274-306
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes out the source map in the following format (line numbers are for
   * reference only and are not part of the format):
   *
   * 1.  {
   * 2.    version: 3,
   * 3.    file: "out.js",
   * 4.    lineCount: 2,
   * 5.    sourceRoot: "",
   * 6.    sources: ["foo.js", "bar.js"],
   * 7.    names: ["src", "maps", "are", "fun"],
   * 8.    mappings: "a;;abcde,abcd,a;"
   * 9.  }
   *
   * Line 1: The entire file is a single JSON object
   * Line 2: File revision (always the first entry in the object)
   * Line 3: The name of the file that this source map is associated with.
   * Line 4: The number of lines represented in the sourcemap.
   * Line 5: An optional source root, useful for relocating source files on a
   *     server or removing repeated prefix values in the "sources" entry.
   * Line 6: A list of sources used by the "mappings" entry relative to the
   *     sourceRoot.
   * Line 7: A list of symbol names used by the "mapping" entry.  This list
   *     may be incomplete.
   * Line 8: The mappings field.
   */
Method code:
274 |   @Override
275 |   public void appendTo(Appendable out, String name) throws IOException {
276 |     int maxLine = prepMappings();
277 | 
278 |     // Add the header fields.
279 |     out.append("{\n");
280 |     appendFirstField(out, "version", "3");
281 |     appendField(out, "file", escapeString(name));
282 |     appendField(out, "lineCount", String.valueOf(maxLine + 1));
283 | 
284 |     // Add the mappings themselves.
285 |     appendFieldStart(out, "mappings");
286 |     // out.append("[");
287 |     (new LineMapper(out)).appendLineMappings();
288 |     // out.append("]");
289 |     appendFieldEnd(out);
290 | 
291 |     // Files names
292 |     appendFieldStart(out, "sources");
293 |     out.append("[");
294 |     addSourceNameMap(out);
295 |     out.append("]");
296 |     appendFieldEnd(out);
297 | 
298 |     // Files names
299 |     appendFieldStart(out, "names");
300 |     out.append("[");
301 |     addSymbolNameMap(out);
302 |     out.append("]");
303 |     appendFieldEnd(out);
304 | 
305 |     out.append("\n}\n");
306 |   }

===== M0338 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 509-531
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An rvalue in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
509 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
510 |       switch (n.getType()) {
511 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
512 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
513 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
514 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
515 |         case Token.OR:
516 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
517 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
518 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
519 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
520 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
521 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
522 |         case Token.HOOK:
523 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
524 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
525 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
526 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) return t;
527 |           Node third = second.getNext();
528 |           return getValueType(third);
529 |       }
530 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
531 |     }

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x && !isNegativeZero(x)) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0364 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 84-106
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
85 |     try {
86 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
87 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
88 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
89 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
90 |           logger_);
91 |     } catch (IOException e) {
92 |       compiler.report(
93 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.SCRIPT);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 |

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */

471 |          current != null;

473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

===== M0378 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

===== M0422 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1153-1291
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1153 |   Node parseInputs() {
1154 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1155 | 
1156 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1157 |     // individual file parse trees.
1158 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1159 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1160 |     }
1161 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1162 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1163 |     }
1164 | 
1165 |     // Parse main js sources.
1166 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1167 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1168 | 
1169 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1170 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1171 | 
1172 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1173 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1174 | 
1175 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1176 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1177 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1178 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1179 |     }
1180 | 
1181 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1182 | 
1183 |     try {
1184 |       // Parse externs sources.
1185 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1186 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1187 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1188 |           return null;
1189 |         }
1190 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1191 |       }
1192 | 
1193 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1194 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1195 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1196 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1197 | 
1198 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1199 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1200 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1201 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1202 |           }
1203 |         }
1204 | 
1205 |         try {
1206 |           inputs =
1207 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1208 |               .manageDependencies(
1209 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1210 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1211 |           report(JSError.make(
1212 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1213 |           return null;
1214 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1215 |           report(JSError.make(
1216 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1217 |           return null;
1218 |         }
1219 |       }
1220 | 
1221 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1222 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1223 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1224 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1225 | 
1226 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1227 |         if (n == null) {
1228 |           continue;
1229 |         }
1230 | 
1231 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1232 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1233 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1234 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1235 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1236 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1237 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1238 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1239 | 
1240 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1241 | 
1242 |             externs.add(input);
1243 |             staleInputs = true;
1244 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1245 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1246 |             staleInputs = true;
1247 |           }
1248 |         }
1249 |       }
1250 | 
1251 |       if (staleInputs) {
1252 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1253 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1254 |       }
1255 | 
1256 |       // Build the AST.
1257 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1258 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1259 |         if (n == null) {
1260 |           continue;
1261 |         }
1262 | 
1263 |         if (devMode) {
1264 |           runSanityCheck();
1265 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1266 |             return null;
1267 |           }
1268 |         }
1269 | 
1270 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1271 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1272 | 
1273 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1274 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1275 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1276 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1277 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1278 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1279 |         }
1280 | 
1281 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1282 |       }
1283 | 
1284 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1285 |         return null;
1286 |       }
1287 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1288 |     } finally {
1289 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1290 |     }
1291 |   }

===== M0427 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 429-445
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
429 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
430 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
431 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
432 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
433 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
434 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
435 |         String inputName = input.getName();
436 | 
437 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
438 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
439 |         // will get an error down the line.
440 |         inputs.add(input);
441 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
442 |       }
443 |     }
444 |     return inputs;
445 |   }

===== M0438 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 937-964
Documentation:

Method code:
937 |   String jsString(String s) {
938 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
939 | 
940 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
941 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
942 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
943 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
944 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
945 |       }
946 |     }
947 | 
948 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
949 |     char quote;
950 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
951 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
952 |       quote = '\'';
953 |       doublequote = "\"";
954 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
955 |     } else {
956 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
957 |       quote = '\"';
958 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
959 |       singlequote = "\'";
960 |     }
961 | 
962 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
963 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
964 |   }

Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.Base64VLQ.encode(Appendable,int)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/Base64VLQ.java
Original range: 87-98
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes a VLQ encoded value to the provide appendable.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
87 |   public static void encode(Appendable out, int value)
88 |       throws IOException {
89 |     value = toVLQSigned(value);
90 |     do {
91 |       int digit = value & VLQ_BASE_MASK;
92 |       value >>>= VLQ_BASE_SHIFT;
93 |       if (value > 0) {
94 |         digit |= VLQ_CONTINUATION_BIT;
95 |       }
96 |       out.append(Base64.toBase64(digit));
97 |     } while (value > 0);
98 |   }

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 578-606
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment to a name,
     * a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set
     * @param parent Parent node of {@code n} (an ASSIGN, VAR, or OBJLIT node)
     * @return Whether the set operation is either a constructor or enum
     *     declaration
     */
Method code:
578 |     private boolean isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node n, Node parent) {
579 |       JSDocInfo info;
580 |       int valueNodeType;
581 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
582 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
583 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
584 |           valueNodeType = n.getNext().getType();
585 |           break;
586 |         case Token.VAR:
587 |           info = n.getJSDocInfo();
588 |           if (info == null) {
589 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
590 |           }
591 |           Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
592 |           valueNodeType = valueNode != null ? valueNode.getType() : Token.VOID;
593 |           break;
594 |         default:
595 |           if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) {
596 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
597 |             valueNodeType = Token.FUNCTION;
598 |             break;
599 |           }
600 |           return false;
601 |       }
602 |       // Heed the annotations only if they're sensibly used.
603 |       return info != null &&
604 |              (info.isConstructor() && valueNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
605 |               info.hasEnumParameterType() && valueNodeType == Token.OBJECTLIT);
606 |     }

163 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
164 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,

167 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
168 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SourceMap.fixupSourceLocation(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SourceMap.java
Original range: 149-175
Documentation:
/**
   * @param sourceFile The source file location to fixup.
   * @return a remapped source file.
   */
Method code:
149 |   private String fixupSourceLocation(String sourceFile) {
150 |     if (prefixMappings.isEmpty()) {
151 |       return sourceFile;
152 |     }
153 | 
154 |     String fixed = sourceLocationFixupCache.get(sourceFile);
155 |     if (fixed != null) {
156 |       return fixed;
157 |     }
158 | 
159 |     // Replace the first prefix found with its replacement
160 |     for (LocationMapping mapping : prefixMappings) {
161 |       if (sourceFile.startsWith(mapping.prefix)) {
162 |         fixed = mapping.replacement + sourceFile.substring(
163 |           mapping.prefix.length());
164 |         break;
165 |       }
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // If none of the mappings match then use the original file path.
169 |     if (fixed == null) {
170 |       fixed = sourceFile;
171 |     }
172 | 
173 |     sourceLocationFixupCache.put(sourceFile, fixed);
174 |     return fixed;
175 |   }

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilerOptions.getReplacementsHelper(Map<String, Object>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilerOptions.java
Original range: 917-936
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a map of String->Node from a map of String->Number/String/Boolean.
   */
Method code:
917 |   private static Map<String, Node> getReplacementsHelper(
918 |       Map<String, Object> source) {
919 |     Map<String, Node> map = Maps.newHashMap();
920 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : source.entrySet()) {
921 |       String name = entry.getKey();
922 |       Object value = entry.getValue();
923 |       if (value instanceof Boolean) {
924 |         map.put(name, ((Boolean) value).booleanValue() ?
925 |             new Node(Token.TRUE) : new Node(Token.FALSE));
926 |       } else if (value instanceof Integer) {
927 |         map.put(name, Node.newNumber(((Integer) value).intValue()));
928 |       } else if (value instanceof Double) {
929 |         map.put(name, Node.newNumber(((Double) value).doubleValue()));
930 |       } else {
931 |         Preconditions.checkState(value instanceof String);
932 |         map.put(name, Node.newString((String) value));
933 |       }
934 |     }
935 |     return map;
936 |   }
