/**
   * Appends the index source map to the given buffer.
   *
   * @param out The stream to which the map will be appended.
   * @param name The name of the generated source file that this source map
   *   represents.
   * @param sections An ordered list of map sections to include in the index.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
610 |   @Override
611 |   public void appendIndexMapTo(
612 |       Appendable out, String name, List<SourceMapSection> sections)
613 |       throws IOException {
614 |     // Add the header fields.
615 |     out.append("{\n");
616 |     appendFirstField(out, "version", "3");
617 |     appendField(out, "file", escapeString(name));
618 | 
619 |     // Add the line character maps.

627 |       } else {
628 |         out.append(",\n");
629 |       }
630 |       out.append("{\n");
631 |       appendFirstField(out, "offset",
632 |           offsetValue(section.getLine(), section.getColumn()));
633 |       if (section.getSectionType() == SourceMapSection.SectionType.URL) {
634 |         appendField(out, "url", escapeString(section.getSectionValue()));
635 |       } else if (section.getSectionType() == SourceMapSection.SectionType.MAP) {
636 |         appendField(out, "map", section.getSectionValue());
637 |       } else {
638 |         throw new IOException("Unexpected section type");
639 |       }
640 |       out.append("\n}");
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     out.append("\n]");
644 |     appendFieldEnd(out);
645 | 
646 |     out.append("\n}\n");
647 |   }

===== M0510 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapConsumerV3.parseMetaMap(JSONObject,SourceMapSupplier)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapConsumerV3.java
Original range: 136-203
Documentation:
/**
   * @param sourceMapRoot
   * @throws SourceMapParseException
   */
Method code:
136 |   private void parseMetaMap(
137 |       JSONObject sourceMapRoot, SourceMapSupplier sectionSupplier)
138 |       throws SourceMapParseException {
139 |     if (sectionSupplier == null) {
140 |       sectionSupplier = new DefaultSourceMapSupplier();
141 |     }
142 | 
143 |     try {
144 |       // Check basic assertions about the format.
145 |       int version = sourceMapRoot.getInt("version");
146 |       if (version != 3) {
147 |         throw new SourceMapParseException("Unknown version: " + version);
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       String file = sourceMapRoot.getString("file");
151 |       if (file.isEmpty()) {
152 |         throw new SourceMapParseException("File entry is missing or empty");
153 |       }
154 | 
155 |       if (sourceMapRoot.has("lineCount")
156 |           || sourceMapRoot.has("mappings")
157 |           || sourceMapRoot.has("sources")
158 |           || sourceMapRoot.has("names")) {
159 |         throw new SourceMapParseException("Invalid map format");
160 |       }
161 | 
162 |       SourceMapGeneratorV3 generator = new SourceMapGeneratorV3();
163 |       JSONArray sections = sourceMapRoot.getJSONArray("sections");
164 |       for (int i = 0, count = sections.length(); i < count; i++) {
165 |         JSONObject section = sections.getJSONObject(i);
166 |         if (section.has("map") && section.has("url")) {
167 |           throw new SourceMapParseException(
168 |               "Invalid map format: section may not have both 'map' and 'url'");
169 |         }
170 |         JSONObject offset = section.getJSONObject("offset");
171 |         int line = offset.getInt("line");
172 |         int column = offset.getInt("column");
173 |         String mapSectionContents;
174 |         if (section.has("url")) {
175 |           String url = section.getString("url");
176 |           mapSectionContents = sectionSupplier.getSourceMap(url);
177 |           if (mapSectionContents == null) {
178 |             throw new SourceMapParseException("Unable to retrieve: " + url);
179 |           }
180 |         } else if (section.has("map")) {
181 |           mapSectionContents = section.getString("map");
182 |         } else {
183 |           throw new SourceMapParseException(
184 |               "Invalid map format: section must have either 'map' or 'url'");
185 |         }
186 |         generator.mergeMapSection(line, column, mapSectionContents);
187 |       }
188 | 
189 |       StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
190 |       try {
191 |         generator.appendTo(sb, file);
192 |       } catch (IOException e) {
193 |         // Can't happen.
194 |         throw new RuntimeException(e);
195 |       }
196 | 
197 |       parse(sb.toString());
198 |     } catch (IOException ex) {
199 |       throw new SourceMapParseException("IO exception: " + ex);
200 |     } catch (JSONException ex) {
201 |       throw new SourceMapParseException("JSON parse exception: " + ex);
202 |     }
203 |   }

===== M0511 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0513 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 986-1056
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
986 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
987 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
988 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
989 |                           String backslashEscape,
990 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
991 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
992 |     sb.append(quote);
993 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
994 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
995 |       switch (c) {
996 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
997 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
998 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
999 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1000 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1001 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1002 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1003 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1004 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1005 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1006 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1007 |             sb.append("\\>");
1008 |           } else {
1009 |             sb.append(c);
1010 |           }
1011 |           break;
1012 |         case '<':
1013 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1014 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1015 | 
1016 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1017 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1018 | 
1019 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1020 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1021 |             sb.append("<\\");
1022 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1023 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1024 |             sb.append("<\\");
1025 |           } else {
1026 |             sb.append(c);
1027 |           }
1028 |           break;
1029 |         default:
1030 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1031 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1032 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1033 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1034 |               sb.append(c);
1035 |             } else {
1036 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1037 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1038 |             }
1039 |           } else {
1040 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1041 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1042 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1043 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1044 |               sb.append(c);
1045 |             } else {
1046 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1047 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1048 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1049 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1050 |             }
1051 |           }
1052 |       }
1053 |     }
1054 |     sb.append(quote);
1055 |     return sb.toString();
1056 |   }

===== M0524 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);

===== M0545 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0546 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

Original range: 243-273
Documentation:

Method code:
243 |     @Override
244 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
245 |       if (n.isName()) {
246 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
247 |           case Token.VAR:
248 |           case Token.FUNCTION:
249 |           case Token.PARAM_LIST:
250 |             // These are okay.
251 |             break;
252 |           case Token.GETPROP:
253 |             if (n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
254 |               Scope scope = t.getScope();
255 |               Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(n.getString());
256 |               if (var == null) {
257 |                 t.report(n, UNDEFINED_EXTERN_VAR_ERROR, n.getString());
258 |                 varsToDeclareInExterns.add(n.getString());
259 |               }
260 |             }
261 |             break;
262 |           default:
263 |             t.report(n, NAME_REFERENCE_IN_EXTERNS_ERROR, n.getString());
264 | 
265 |             Scope scope = t.getScope();
266 |             Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(n.getString());
267 |             if (var == null) {
268 |               varsToDeclareInExterns.add(n.getString());
269 |             }
270 |             break;
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     }

===== M0588 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

2841 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2842 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
2843 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String,boolean,Name.Type)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 545-566
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect an
     * assignment to a global name in global scope.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @param isPropAssign Whether this set corresponds to a property
     *     assignment of the form <code>a.b.c = ...;</code>
     * @param type The type of the value that the name is being assigned
     */
Method code:
545 |     void handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name,
546 |                              boolean isPropAssign, Name.Type type) {
547 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
548 | 
549 |       Name nameObj = getOrCreateName(name);
550 |       nameObj.type = type;
551 | 
552 |       Ref set = new Ref(t, n, nameObj, Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL,
553 |           currentPreOrderIndex++);
554 |       nameObj.addRef(set);
555 | 
556 |       if (isNestedAssign(parent)) {
557 |         // This assignment is both a set and a get that creates an alias.
558 |         Ref get = new Ref(t, n, nameObj, Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET,
559 |             currentPreOrderIndex++);
560 |         nameObj.addRef(get);
561 |         Ref.markTwins(set, get);
562 |       } else if (isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(n, parent)) {
563 |         // Names with a @constructor or @enum annotation are always collapsed
564 |         nameObj.setIsClassOrEnum();
565 |       }
566 |     }

   * Parses the given contents containing a source map.

Method code:
98 |   public void parse(JSONObject sourceMapRoot, SourceMapSupplier sectionSupplier)
99 |       throws SourceMapParseException {
100 |     try {
101 |       // Check basic assertions about the format.
102 |       int version = sourceMapRoot.getInt("version");
103 |       if (version != 3) {
104 |         throw new SourceMapParseException("Unknown version: " + version);
105 |       }
106 |

108 |       if (file.isEmpty()) {
109 |         throw new SourceMapParseException("File entry is missing or empty");
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       if (sourceMapRoot.has("sections")) {
113 |         // Looks like a index map, try to parse it that way.
114 |         parseMetaMap(sourceMapRoot, sectionSupplier);
115 |         return;
116 |       }
117 | 
118 |       lineCount = sourceMapRoot.getInt("lineCount");
119 |       String lineMap = sourceMapRoot.getString("mappings");
120 | 
121 |       sources = getJavaStringArray(sourceMapRoot.getJSONArray("sources"));
122 |       names = getJavaStringArray(sourceMapRoot.getJSONArray("names"));
123 | 
124 |       lines = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(lineCount);
125 | 
126 |       new MappingBuilder(lineMap).build();
127 |     } catch (JSONException ex) {
128 |       throw new SourceMapParseException("JSON parse exception: " + ex);
129 |     }
130 |   }

===== M0603 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0609 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2427-2437
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2427 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2428 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2429 |     int len = s.length();
2430 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2431 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2432 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2433 |         return false;
2434 |       }
2435 |     }
2436 |     return true;
2437 |   }

===== M0614 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractBlockComment(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1429-1474
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1429 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1430 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1431 | 
1432 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1433 | 
1434 |     do {
1435 |       switch (token) {
1436 |         case ANNOTATION:
1437 |         case EOC:
1438 |         case EOF:
1439 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1440 | 
1441 |         case STAR:
1442 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1443 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1444 |               builder.append(' ');
1445 |             }
1446 | 
1447 |             builder.append('*');
1448 |           }
1449 | 
1450 |           token = next();
1451 |           continue;
1452 | 
1453 |         case EOL:
1454 |           ignoreStar = true;
1455 |           builder.append('\n');
1456 |           token = next();
1457 |           continue;
1458 | 
1459 |         default:
1460 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1461 |             builder.append(' ');
1462 |           }
1463 | 
1464 |           ignoreStar = false;
1465 | 
1466 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1467 | 
1468 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1469 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1470 |           builder.append(line);
1471 |           token = next();
1472 |       }
1473 |     } while (true);
1474 |   }

Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.Base64VLQ.fromVLQSigned(int)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/Base64VLQ.java
Original range: 77-81
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts to a two-complement value from a value where the sign bit is
   * is placed in the least significant bit.  For example, as decimals:
   *   2 (10 binary) becomes 1, 3 (11 binary) becomes -1
   *   4 (100 binary) becomes 2, 5 (101 binary) becomes -2
   */
Method code:
77 |   private static int fromVLQSigned(int value) {
78 |     boolean negate = (value & 1) == 1;
79 |     value = value >> 1;
80 |     return negate ? -value : value;
81 |   }

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1159 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1160 |     switch (type) {
1161 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1162 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1163 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1174 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1175 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1176 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1177 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1178 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1179 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1180 |       case Token.EQ:
1181 |       case Token.NE:
1182 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1183 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1184 |       case Token.LT:
1185 |       case Token.GT:
1186 |       case Token.LE:
1187 |       case Token.GE:
1188 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1189 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1190 |       case Token.LSH:
1191 |       case Token.RSH:
1192 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1193 |       case Token.SUB:
1194 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1195 |       case Token.MUL:
1196 |       case Token.MOD:
1197 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1198 |       case Token.INC:
1199 |       case Token.DEC:

1223 |       case Token.THIS:
1224 |       case Token.TRUE:

1226 |

1228 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1229 |                                " (type " + type + ")");

===== M0627 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 286-371
Documentation:

Method code:
286 |     @Override
287 |     public  void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
288 |       RefInfo refInfo = allRefInfo.get(n);
289 |       if (refInfo != null) {
290 |         Ref ref = refInfo.ref;
291 |         Name name = refInfo.name;
292 |         String fullName = name.getFullName();
293 |         switch (ref.type) {
294 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
295 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
296 |             Node valParent = getValueParent(ref);
297 |             Node val = valParent.getLastChild();
298 |             if (valParent.isAssign() && name.isSimpleName() &&
299 |                 name.getDeclaration() == ref) {
300 |               // For defines, it's an error if a simple name is assigned
301 |               // before it's declared
302 |               compiler.report(
303 |                   t.makeError(val, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, fullName));
304 |             } else if (processDefineAssignment(t, fullName, val, valParent)) {
305 |               // remove the assignment so that the variable is still declared,
306 |               // but no longer assigned to a value, e.g.,
307 |               // DEF_FOO = 5; // becomes "5;"
308 | 
309 |               // We can't remove the ASSIGN/VAR when we're still visiting its
310 |               // children, so we'll have to come back later to remove it.
311 |               refInfo.name.removeRef(ref);
312 |               lvalueToRemoveLater = valParent;
313 |             }
314 |             break;
315 |           default:
316 |             if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
317 |               // Treat this as a reference to a define in the global scope.
318 |               // After this point, the define must not be reassigned,
319 |               // or it's an error.
320 |               DefineInfo info = assignableDefines.get(fullName);
321 |               if (info != null) {
322 |                 setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
323 |                 assignableDefines.remove(fullName);
324 |               }
325 |             }
326 |             break;
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope() &&
331 |           n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isDefine()) {
332 |         // warn about @define annotations in local scopes
333 |         compiler.report(
334 |             t.makeError(n, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, ""));
335 |       }
336 | 
337 |       if (lvalueToRemoveLater == n) {
338 |         lvalueToRemoveLater = null;
339 |         if (n.isAssign()) {
340 |           Node last = n.getLastChild();
341 |           n.removeChild(last);
342 |           parent.replaceChild(n, last);
343 |         } else {
344 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.isName());
345 |           n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild());
346 |         }
347 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       if (n.isCall()) {
351 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
352 |           // If there's a function call in the global scope,
353 |           // we just say it's unsafe and freeze all the defines.
354 |           //
355 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): We could be a lot smarter here. For example,
356 |           // ReplaceOverriddenVars keeps a call graph of all functions and
357 |           // which functions/variables that they reference, and tries
358 |           // to statically determine which functions are "safe" and which
359 |           // are not. But this would be overkill, expecially because
360 |           // the intended use of defines is with config_files, where
361 |           // all the defines are at the top of the bundle.
362 |           for (DefineInfo info : assignableDefines.values()) {
363 |             setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
364 |           }
365 | 
366 |           assignableDefines.clear();
367 |         }
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       updateAssignAllowedStack(n, false);
371 |     }

===== M0634 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 459-475
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();

===== M0639 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapGeneratorV3.MappingTraversal.traverse(MappingVisitor)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapGeneratorV3.java
Original range: 488-517
Documentation:
// Append the line mapping entries.
Method code:
488 |     void traverse(MappingVisitor v) throws IOException {
489 |       // The mapping list is ordered as a pre-order traversal.  The mapping
490 |       // positions give us enough information to rebuild the stack and this
491 |       // allows the building of the source map in O(n) time.
492 |       Deque<Mapping> stack = new ArrayDeque<Mapping>();
493 |       for (Mapping m : mappings) {
494 |         // Find the closest ancestor of the current mapping:
495 |         // An overlapping mapping is an ancestor of the current mapping, any
496 |         // non-overlapping mappings are siblings (or cousins) and must be
497 |         // closed in the reverse order of when they encountered.
498 |         while (!stack.isEmpty() && !isOverlapped(stack.peek(), m)) {
499 |           Mapping previous = stack.pop();
500 |           maybeVisit(v, previous);
501 |         }
502 | 
503 |         // Any gaps between the current line position and the start of the
504 |         // current mapping belong to the parent.
505 |         Mapping parent = stack.peek();
506 |         maybeVisitParent(v, parent, m);
507 | 
508 |         stack.push(m);
509 |       }
510 | 
511 |       // There are no more children to be had, simply close the remaining
512 |       // mappings in the reverse order of when they encountered.
513 |       while (!stack.isEmpty()) {
514 |         Mapping m = stack.pop();
515 |         maybeVisit(v, m);
516 |       }
517 |     }

===== M0640 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.CodeBuilder.append(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1570-1589
Documentation:
/** Appends the given string to the text buffer. */
Method code:
1570 |     CodeBuilder append(String str) {
1571 |       sb.append(str);
1572 | 
1573 |       // Adjust the line and column information for the new text.
1574 |       int index = -1;
1575 |       int lastIndex = index;
1576 |       while ((index = str.indexOf('\n', index + 1)) >= 0) {
1577 |         ++lineCount;
1578 |         lastIndex = index;
1579 |       }
1580 | 
1581 |       if (lastIndex == -1) {
1582 |         // No new lines, append the new characters added.
1583 |         colCount += str.length();
1584 |       } else {
1585 |         colCount = str.length() - (lastIndex + 1);
1586 |       }
1587 | 
1588 |       return this;
1589 |     }

===== M0648 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapConsumerV3.search(ArrayList<Entry>,int,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapConsumerV3.java
Original range: 435-455
Documentation:
/**
   * Perform a binary search on the array to find a section that covers
   * the target column.
   */
Method code:
435 |   private int search(ArrayList<Entry> entries, int target, int start, int end) {
436 |     while (true) {
437 |       int mid = ((end - start) / 2) + start;
438 |       int compare = compareEntry(entries, mid, target);
439 |       if (compare == 0) {
440 |         return mid;
441 |       } else if (compare < 0) {
442 |         // it is in the upper half
443 |         start = mid + 1;
444 |         if (start > end) {
445 |           return end;
446 |         }
447 |       } else {
448 |         // it is in the lower half
449 |         end = mid - 1;
450 |         if (end < start) {
451 |           return end;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |     }
455 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1217 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1218 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1219 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1221 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1222 |       } else {
1223 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1224 |       }
1225 |     }
1226 |     return builder.build();
1227 |   }

   * Writes out js code from a root node. If printing input delimiters, this
   * method will attach a comment to the start of the text indicating which
   * input the output derived from. If there were any preserve annotations
   * within the root's source, they will also be printed in a block comment
   * at the beginning of the output.
   */
Method code:
1461 |   public void toSource(final CodeBuilder cb,
1462 |                        final int inputSeqNum,
1463 |                        final Node root) {
1464 |     runInCompilerThread(new Callable<Void>() {
1465 |       @Override
1466 |       public Void call() throws Exception {
1467 |         if (options.printInputDelimiter) {
1468 |           if ((cb.getLength() > 0) && !cb.endsWith("\n")) {
1469 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line
1470 |           }
1471 |           Preconditions.checkState(root.isScript());
1472 | 
1473 |           String delimiter = options.inputDelimiter;
1474 | 
1475 |           String inputName = root.getInputId().getIdName();
1476 |           String sourceName = root.getSourceFileName();
1477 |           Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
1478 |           Preconditions.checkState(!sourceName.isEmpty());
1479 | 
1480 |           delimiter = delimiter
1481 |               .replaceAll("%name%", Matcher.quoteReplacement(inputName))
1482 |               .replaceAll("%num%", String.valueOf(inputSeqNum));
1483 | 
1484 |           cb.append(delimiter)
1485 |             .append("\n");
1486 |         }
1487 |         if (root.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
1488 |             root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null) {
1489 |           cb.append("/*\n")
1490 |             .append(root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense())
1491 |             .append("*/\n");
1492 |         }
1493 | 
1494 |         // If there is a valid source map, then indicate to it that the current
1495 |         // root node's mappings are offset by the given string builder buffer.
1496 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
1497 |           sourceMap.setStartingPosition(
1498 |               cb.getLineIndex(), cb.getColumnIndex());
1499 |         }
1500 | 
1501 |         // if LanguageMode is ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT, only print 'use strict'
1502 |         // for the first input file
1503 |         String code = toSource(root, sourceMap, inputSeqNum == 0);
1504 |         if (!code.isEmpty()) {
1505 |           cb.append(code);
1506 | 
1507 |           // In order to avoid parse ambiguity when files are concatenated
1508 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1509 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1510 |           int length = code.length();
1511 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1512 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1513 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1514 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1515 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1516 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1517 |             cb.append(";");
1518 |           }
1519 |         }
1520 |         return null;
1521 |       }
1522 |     });
1523 |   }

Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapGeneratorV3.addMapping(String,String,FilePosition,FilePosition,FilePosition)
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapGeneratorV3.java
Original range: 162-226
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a mapping for the given node.  Mappings must be added in order.
   * @param startPosition The position on the starting line
   * @param endPosition The position on the ending line.
   */
Method code:
162 |   @Override
163 |   public void addMapping(
164 |       String sourceName, @Nullable String symbolName,
165 |       FilePosition sourceStartPosition,
166 |       FilePosition startPosition, FilePosition endPosition) {
167 | 
168 |     // Don't bother if there is not sufficient information to be useful.
169 |     if (sourceName == null || sourceStartPosition.getLine() < 0) {
170 |       return;
171 |     }
172 | 
173 |     FilePosition adjustedStart = startPosition;
174 |     FilePosition adjustedEnd = endPosition;
175 | 
176 |     if (offsetPosition.getLine() != 0
177 |         || offsetPosition.getColumn() != 0) {
178 |       // If the mapping is found on the first line, we need to offset
179 |       // its character position by the number of characters found on
180 |       // the *last* line of the source file to which the code is
181 |       // being generated.
182 |       int offsetLine = offsetPosition.getLine();
183 |       int startOffsetPosition = offsetPosition.getColumn();
184 |       int endOffsetPosition = offsetPosition.getColumn();
185 | 
186 |       if (startPosition.getLine() > 0) {
187 |         startOffsetPosition = 0;
188 |       }
189 | 
190 |       if (endPosition.getLine() > 0) {
191 |         endOffsetPosition = 0;
192 |       }
193 | 
194 |       adjustedStart = new FilePosition(
195 |           startPosition.getLine() + offsetLine,
196 |           startPosition.getColumn() + startOffsetPosition);
197 | 
198 |       adjustedEnd = new FilePosition(
199 |           endPosition.getLine() + offsetLine,
200 |           endPosition.getColumn() + endOffsetPosition);
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     // Create the new mapping.
204 |     Mapping mapping = new Mapping();
205 |     mapping.sourceFile = sourceName;
206 |     mapping.originalPosition = sourceStartPosition;
207 |     mapping.originalName = symbolName;
208 |     mapping.startPosition = adjustedStart;
209 |     mapping.endPosition = adjustedEnd;
210 | 
211 |     // Validate the mappings are in a proper order.
212 |     if (lastMapping != null) {
213 |       int lastLine = lastMapping.startPosition.getLine();
214 |       int lastColumn = lastMapping.startPosition.getColumn();
215 |       int nextLine = mapping.startPosition.getLine();
216 |       int nextColumn = mapping.startPosition.getColumn();
217 |       Preconditions.checkState(nextLine > lastLine
218 |           || (nextLine == lastLine && nextColumn >= lastColumn),
219 |           "Incorrect source mappings order, previous : (%s,%s)\n"
220 |           + "new : (%s,%s)\nnode : %s",
221 |           lastLine, lastColumn, nextLine, nextColumn);
222 |     }
223 | 
224 |     lastMapping = mapping;
225 |     mappings.add(mapping);
226 |   }

===== M0713 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.collectDefines(Node,GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 172-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds all defines, and creates a {@link DefineInfo} data structure for
   * each one.
   * @return A map of {@link DefineInfo} structures, keyed by name.
   */
Method code:
172 |   private Map<String, DefineInfo> collectDefines(Node root,
173 |       GlobalNamespace namespace) {
174 |     // Find all the global names with a @define annotation
175 |     List<Name> allDefines = Lists.newArrayList();
176 |     for (Name name : namespace.getNameIndex().values()) {
177 |       Ref decl = name.getDeclaration();
178 |       if (name.docInfo != null && name.docInfo.isDefine()) {
179 |         // Process defines should not depend on check types being enabled,
180 |         // so we look for the JSDoc instead of the inferred type.
181 |         if (isValidDefineType(name.docInfo.getType())) {
182 |           allDefines.add(name);
183 |         } else {
184 |           JSError error = JSError.make(
185 |               decl.getSourceName(),
186 |               decl.node, INVALID_DEFINE_TYPE_ERROR);
187 |           compiler.report(error);
188 |         }
189 |       } else {
190 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
191 |           if (ref == decl) {
192 |             // Declarations were handled above.
193 |             continue;
194 |           }
195 | 
196 |           Node n = ref.node;
197 |           Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
198 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
199 |           if (info == null &&
200 |               parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
201 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
202 |           }
203 | 
204 |           if (info != null && info.isDefine()) {
205 |             allDefines.add(name);
206 |             break;
207 |           }
208 |         }
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     CollectDefines pass = new CollectDefines(compiler, allDefines);
213 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, pass);
214 |     return pass.getAllDefines();
215 |   }

     * decode the relative values.
     *
     * @param vals An array of integers that represent values in the entry.
     * @param entryValues The number of entries in the array.
     * @return The entry object.
     */
Method code:
349 |     private Entry decodeEntry(int[] vals, int entryValues) {
350 |       Entry entry;
351 |       switch (entryValues) {
352 |         // The first values, if present are in the following order:
353 |         //   0: the starting column in the current line of the generated file
354 |         //   1: the id of the original source file
355 |         //   2: the starting line in the original source
356 |         //   3: the starting column in the original source
357 |         //   4: the id of the original symbol name
358 |         // The values are relative to the last encountered value for that field.
359 |         // Note: the previously column value for the generated file is reset
360 |         // to '0' when a new line is encountered.  This is done in the 'build'
361 |         // method.
362 | 
363 |         case 1:
364 |           // An unmapped section of the generated file.
365 |           entry = new UnmappedEntry(
366 |               vals[0] + previousCol);
367 |           // Set the values see for the next entry.
368 |           previousCol = entry.getGeneratedColumn();
369 |           return entry;
370 | 
371 |         case 4:
372 |           // A mapped section of the generated file.
373 |           entry = new UnnamedEntry(
374 |               vals[0] + previousCol,
375 |               vals[1] + previousSrcId,
376 |               vals[2] + previousSrcLine,
377 |               vals[3] + previousSrcColumn);
378 |           // Set the values see for the next entry.
379 |           previousCol = entry.getGeneratedColumn();
380 |           previousSrcId = entry.getSourceFileId();
381 |           previousSrcLine = entry.getSourceLine();
382 |           previousSrcColumn = entry.getSourceColumn();
383 |           return entry;
384 | 
385 |         case 5:
386 |           // A mapped section of the generated file, that has an associated
387 |           // name.
388 |           entry = new NamedEntry(
389 |               vals[0] + previousCol,
390 |               vals[1] + previousSrcId,
391 |               vals[2] + previousSrcLine,
392 |               vals[3] + previousSrcColumn,
393 |               vals[4] + previousNameId);
394 |           // Set the values see for the next entry.
395 |           previousCol = entry.getGeneratedColumn();
396 |           previousSrcId = entry.getSourceFileId();
397 |           previousSrcLine = entry.getSourceLine();
398 |           previousSrcColumn = entry.getSourceColumn();
399 |           previousNameId = entry.getNameId();
400 |           return entry;
401 | 
402 |         default:
403 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
404 |               "Unexpected number of values for entry:" + entryValues);
405 |       }
406 |     }

===== M0717 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapConsumerV3.MappingBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/debugging/sourcemap/SourceMapConsumerV3.java
Original range: 292-328
Documentation:

Method code:
292 |     void build() {
293 |       int [] temp = new int[MAX_ENTRY_VALUES];
294 |       ArrayList<Entry> entries = new ArrayList<Entry>();
295 |       while (content.hasNext()) {
296 |         // ';' denotes a new line.
297 |         if (tryConsumeToken(';')) {
298 |           // The line is complete, store the result for the line,
299 |           // null if the line is empty.
300 |           ArrayList<Entry> result;
301 |           if (entries.size() > 0) {
302 |             result = entries;
303 |             // A new array list for the next line.
304 |             entries = new ArrayList<Entry>();
305 |           } else {
306 |             result = null;
307 |           }
308 |           lines.add(result);
309 |           entries.clear();
310 |           line++;
311 |           previousCol = 0;
312 |         } else {
313 |           // grab the next entry for the current line.
314 |           int entryValues = 0;
315 |           while (!entryComplete()) {
316 |             temp[entryValues] = nextValue();
317 |             entryValues++;
318 |           }
319 |           Entry entry = decodeEntry(temp, entryValues);
320 | 
321 |           validateEntry(entry);
322 |           entries.add(entry);
323 | 
324 |           // Consume the separating token, if there is one.
325 |           tryConsumeToken(',');
326 |         }
327 |       }
328 |     }

/**
     * Writes an entry for the given column (of the generated text) and
     * associated mapping.
     * The values are stored as relative to the last seen values for each
     * field and encoded as Base64VLQs.

/**
   * @param contents The string representing the source map file contents.
   * @param supplier A supplier for any referenced maps.
   * @return The parsed source map.
   * @throws SourceMapParseException
   */
Method code:
47 |   public static SourceMapping parse(String contents, SourceMapSupplier supplier)
48 |       throws SourceMapParseException {
49 |     // Version 1, starts with a magic string
50 |     if (contents.startsWith("/** Begin line maps. **/")) {
51 |       SourceMapConsumerV1 consumer =  new SourceMapConsumerV1();
52 |       consumer.parse(contents);
53 |       return consumer;
54 |     } else if (contents.startsWith("{")){
55 |       try {
56 |         // Revision 2 and 3, are JSON Objects
57 |         JSONObject sourceMapRoot = new JSONObject(contents);
58 |         // Check basic assertions about the format.
59 |         int version = sourceMapRoot.getInt("version");
60 |         switch (version) {
61 |           case 2: {
62 |             SourceMapConsumerV2 consumer =  new SourceMapConsumerV2();
63 |             consumer.parse(sourceMapRoot);
64 |             return consumer;
65 |           }
66 |           case 3: {
67 |             SourceMapConsumerV3 consumer =  new SourceMapConsumerV3();
68 |             consumer.parse(sourceMapRoot, supplier);
69 |             return consumer;
70 |           }
71 |           default:
72 |             throw new SourceMapParseException(
73 |                 "Unknown source map version:" + version);
74 |         }
75 |       } catch (JSONException ex) {
76 |         throw new SourceMapParseException("JSON parse exception: " + ex);
77 |       }
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     throw new SourceMapParseException("unable to detect source map format");
81 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)

/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

365 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
366 |     if (prototype == null) {
367 |       return false;
368 |     }
369 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
370 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
371 |       return false;

376 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
377 |

379 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
380 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
381 |

383 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
384 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
385 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
386 |     }

392 |       }
393 |

396 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
397 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
398 |           }
399 |         }
400 |       }
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
404 |       clearCachedValues();
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     return true;
408 |   }

/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1167 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1168 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1169 |       return -1;
1170 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1171 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1172 |     } else {
1173 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1174 |     }
1175 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getNearestFunctionName(Node)

Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the function's name. This method recognizes the forms:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code &#123;'name': function() ...&#125;}</li>
   * <li>{@code &#123;name: function() ...&#125;}</li>
   * <li>{@code function name() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @param n a node whose type is {@link Token#FUNCTION}
   * @return the function's name, or {@code null} if it has no name
   */
Method code:
460 |   public static String getNearestFunctionName(Node n) {
461 |     String name = getFunctionName(n);
462 |     if (name != null) {
463 |       return name;
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     // Check for the form { 'x' : function() { } }
467 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
468 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
470 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
471 |       case Token.STRING:
472 |         // Return the name of the literal's key.
473 |         return parent.getString();
474 |       case Token.NUMBER:
475 |         return getStringValue(parent);
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     return null;
479 |   }

/**
     * Gets a {@link Name} instance for a global name. Creates it if necessary,
     * as well as instances for any of its prefixes that are not yet defined.
     *
     * @param name A global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c.d")

===== M0768 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3032-3053
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3032 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3033 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3034 |     if (info == null) {
3035 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3036 |       if (parent == null) {
3037 |         return null;
3038 |       }
3039 | 
3040 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3041 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3042 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3043 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3044 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3045 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3046 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3047 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3048 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3049 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3050 |       }
3051 |     }
3052 |     return info;
3053 |   }

/**
   * Converts from a two-complement value to a value where the sign bit is
   * is placed in the least significant bit.  For example, as decimals:
   *   1 becomes 2 (10 binary), -1 becomes 3 (11 binary)
   *   2 becomes 4 (100 binary), -2 becomes 5 (101 binary)
   */
Method code:
63 |   private static int toVLQSigned(int value) {

===== M0789 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 425-443
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the function's name. This method recognizes five forms:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code function name() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * </ul>
   * In two last cases with named function expressions, the second name is
   * returned (the variable of qualified name).
   *
   * @param n a node whose type is {@link Token#FUNCTION}
   * @return the function's name, or {@code null} if it has no name
   */
Method code:
425 |   static String getFunctionName(Node n) {
426 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
427 |     String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
428 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
429 |       case Token.NAME:
430 |         // var name = function() ...
431 |         // var name2 = function name1() ...
432 |         return parent.getString();
433 | 
434 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
435 |         // qualified.name = function() ...
436 |         // qualified.name2 = function name1() ...
437 |         return parent.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
438 | 
439 |       default:
440 |         // function name() ...
441 |         return name != null && name.length() != 0 ? name : null;
442 |     }
443 |   }

     */

225 |

228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();

234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
235 |

238 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);

===== M0792 =====
Signature: com.google.debugging.sourcemap.SourceMapConsumerV3.getMappingForLine(int,int)

Original range: 205-234
Documentation:

Method code:
205 |   @Override
206 |   public OriginalMapping getMappingForLine(int lineNumber, int column) {
207 |     // Normalize the line and column numbers to 0.
208 |     lineNumber--;
209 |     column--;
210 | 
211 |     if (lineNumber < 0 || lineNumber >= lines.size()) {
212 |       return null;
213 |     }
214 | 
215 |     Preconditions.checkState(lineNumber >= 0);
216 |     Preconditions.checkState(column >= 0);
217 | 
218 | 
219 |     // If the line is empty return the previous mapping.
220 |     if (lines.get(lineNumber) == null) {
221 |       return getPreviousMapping(lineNumber);
222 |     }
223 | 
224 |     ArrayList<Entry> entries = lines.get(lineNumber);
225 |     // No empty lists.
226 |     Preconditions.checkState(entries.size() > 0);
227 |     if (entries.get(0).getGeneratedColumn() > column) {
228 |       return getPreviousMapping(lineNumber);
229 |     }
230 | 
231 |     int index = search(entries, column, 0, entries.size() - 1);
232 |     Preconditions.checkState(index >= 0, "unexpected:%s", index);
233 |     return getOriginalMappingForEntry(entries.get(index));
234 |   }

772 |     if (item == null) {
773 |       return null;
774 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
775 |       return item.getNext();
776 |     } else {
777 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
778 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
779 |         return item.chain(result);
780 |       } else {
781 |         return item;
782 |       }
783 |     }
784 |   }

===== M0801 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 126-213
Documentation:

Method code:
126 |   @Override
127 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
128 |     if (n.getType() != Token.NAME) {
129 |       return;
130 |     }
131 | 
132 |     String varName = n.getString();
133 | 
134 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
135 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
136 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
137 | 
138 |       // A function declaration with an empty name passes Rhino,
139 |       // but is supposed to be a syntax error according to the spec.
140 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
141 |         t.report(n, INVALID_FUNCTION_DECL);
142 |       }
143 |       return;
144 |     }
145 | 
146 |     // Check if this is a declaration for a var that has been declared
147 |     // elsewhere. If so, mark it as a duplicate.
148 |     if ((parent.isVar() ||
149 |          NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) &&
150 |         varsToDeclareInExterns.contains(varName)) {
151 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
152 | 
153 |       n.addSuppression("duplicate");
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
157 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
158 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
159 |     if (var == null) {
160 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
161 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
162 |         // current scope.
163 |       } else {
164 |         // The extern checks are stricter, don't report a second error.
165 |         if (!strictExternCheck || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
166 |           t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
167 |         }
168 | 
169 |         if (sanityCheck) {
170 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
171 |         } else {
172 |           createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
173 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, n,
174 |               null, getSynthesizedExternsInput());
175 |         }
176 |       }
177 |       return;
178 |     }
179 | 
180 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
181 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
182 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
183 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
184 |       return;
185 |     }
186 | 
187 |     // Check module dependencies.
188 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
189 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
190 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
191 |     if (varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
192 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
193 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
194 |       } else {
195 |         if (!sanityCheck && scope.isGlobal()) {
196 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
197 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
198 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
199 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
200 |           } else {
201 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
202 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
203 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.
204 |             t.report(n, MISSING_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
205 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
206 |           }
207 |         } else {
208 |           t.report(n, STRICT_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
209 |                    currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
210 |         }
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 |   }

1349 |       @Override
1350 |       public String call() throws Exception {
1351 |         Tracer tracer = newTracer("toSource");
1352 |         try {
1353 |           CodeBuilder cb = new CodeBuilder();
1354 |           if (jsRoot != null) {
1355 |             int i = 0;
1356 |             for (Node scriptNode = jsRoot.getFirstChild();
1357 |                  scriptNode != null;

1359 |               toSource(cb, i++, scriptNode);
1360 |             }
1361 |           }
1362 |           return cb.toString();
1363 |         } finally {
1364 |           stopTracer(tracer, "toSource");
1365 |         }

===== M0814 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),

62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.

===== M0818 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)

Original range: 310-317
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }
